# wip handoff: the Q-F0 campaign's laptop session (PROPOSAL-m v1.11 §8 step 16, m:358)

Scratch for the laptop session, not of record. Never merged to main. The record is the driver commit on main
(`a6c81f2`) and its two raws, `results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-driver_review.txt` and
`results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-driver_selftest.txt`.

Everything below is laptop-only, under F1 ("Qwen stages launch only when the audit session is idle"), and runs only
on the PI's launch word. The cloud built and reviewed the wiring, and nothing ran on a model.

## 0. What the cloud session left

- **The driver of the campaign:** `scripts/m_sessions.py`, sha256
  `609bb469d97ca40c0f5cc1592902976019f16b2c09b14961fe8352ecaf932015`, committed at `a6c81f2`. Before this
  edit it was `c0a897f5d07be94dee08d37bab16ed76b9f7cc87d1c4929ed49725d44faef4b2`, the driver of record in v1.0 §6 (the
  `qual` wiring, `4630cfd`). Nothing under `valence_core/m` changed: `package_sha256` stays `5f646db6…d771`, and the
  analyzer stays `655e0f7f…c16f`.
  - The step-10 read's Q2 binds this sha: the DRIVER line prints it, and it is recorded "for `campaign`, which follows
    v1.0, [in] the campaign's launch entry".
- **The cloud self-test:** `824/825`. The one failure is environmental: the qwen_snapshot production-defaults check
  needs the Qwen cache, which the cloud lacks. The laptop expects `825/825`. The PI's word said "795/796 expected"
  and "796/796": HEAD's counts (655 module checks and 141 driver checks). The wiring adds 29 driver checks (170).
- **`campaign_dry_precheck.py`** (this branch): the dry precheck, offline and model-free. It makes the driver's pre-load
  checks on the committed driver as `_run_stage` makes them, then prints the DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines. Its docstring
  gives its use. It passes no ruling and no `--resume`, as at a first launch.

## 1. Before the launch (the PI's word first)

1. **The self-test of record:** `python scripts/m_sessions.py --selftest` on the committed driver, in the jlens venv.
   Expect `825/825` with HF_HUB_OFFLINE unset and with it set to "1".
2. **The dry precheck** on the same tree, from the repository root (the snapshot check reads the current directory,
   as the launch line's `cd <repo>` fixes it), in the jlens venv, with HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1: `cd <repo> &&
   HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 <repo>/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python -I -B <this branch>/campaign_dry_precheck.py <repo>
   --analyzer-sha256 655e0f7f6743d0bf497f5ba97ce37f7febf9fcf6a45e068b5385ee945c5dc16f --package-sha256
   5f646db6e82c5561ff8a425e91beb5454068a381564894e93d19b9165459d771 --renders-sha16 a04f67b0e5105a05` (v1.0 §1's
   values; the renders of record's sha16 as lock §0 names it). Every list should print `[]`, and the DRIVER line
   should print `609bb469d97ca40c0f5cc1592902976019f16b2c09b14961fe8352ecaf932015`, the SNAPSHOT line
   `c000ac2c2057d94be3fa931000c31723aac53282`.
3. **m:267's checklist:** power adapter ≥ 96 W on AC; one process (no other model process; the preflight); display
   sleep only; disk headroom; the gate line (the run's GATE line). F1: the audit session idle.

review records it (OPS-7: one line when typed; `<repo>` and the raw's path absolute), the subcommand and the lock's
values being the campaign's:

    nohup caffeinate -is /bin/zsh -c 'cd <repo> && export HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && <repo>/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python
    <repo>/scripts/m_sessions.py campaign --unit Q-F0 --proposal-file PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md

    --package-sha256 5f646db6e82c5561ff8a425e91beb5454068a381564894e93d19b9165459d771 --renders-sha16

this one do not. At G-F0m-Qwen the typed call was followed by `; echo "PID=$!"`, and the header recorded it (the step-14
read: "not a departure"). The header says whatever was run.

## 3. What the campaign invocation does (the driver's terms)

- **Values.** Launch counts A0 128, A0s 64 (a projection, not a bound); floors A0 80, A0s 32 (schedule.FLOORS under
  the locked path); hour cap 40 h; path uncached; on Qwen under LOCK-m-F0 v1.0, on the snapshot of record. The
  committed lock's text must name v1.0 §4's two line forms, "launch counts = A0 128, A0s 64" and "hour cap = 40 h"
  (tested as substrings), as well as the path, the revision of record and the model id.
- **REVISION_STAGES** is `("qual", "campaign")`: a refs/main other than `c000ac2c…3282` is refused before the load.
- **The prior** is G-F0m-Qwen's checkpoint at the same out root (PASS, not on the stub, under this proposal), and
  G-ID-F0m's artifact if on disk.
- **The step-14 read's Q3:** at every STOP and REFUSED print of the stage subcommands (`_run_stage`, through `_say`),
  and in stage_precheck's returned list, a `CHECKPOINT STATE INCONSISTENT` line's two totals are replaced by the
  driver's sentence ("hour_cap_ms_used and the sessions' total differ (both figures redacted at the driver's print:
  the step-14 read, Q3)"). Should that sentence ever print, it "is not quoted or computed against before VERDICTS and
  goes to the seat in a sealed form" (v1.0 §12).
- **Exit codes:**
  - 0: COMPLETE, CAP or PI-STOP;
  - 3: PAUSE (escalated);
  - 4: STOP (escalated), or NOT APPLIED (below);
  - 2: refused before the load (nothing loaded or written);
  - 1: a traceback.
- **The three exit-3 pauses** (`campaign --help` prints them):
  - a fired look's pause resumes with `--resume --adjudication-file <seat's ruling>`, or ends by the PI's stop
    (`--resume --pi-stop-file <PI's text>`);
  - an exhausted schedule's pause ends only by the PI's stop;
  - an end look's pause beside a terminal end (COMPLETE, CAP or PI-STOP) is never resumed. Its ruling enters VERDICTS
    as the analyzer's declared `--adjudication` file (the seat's C9(i)).
  Code never self-adjudicates. A re-invocation with neither ruling nor `--resume` shows the pause (exit 3) and
  launches nothing.
- **The ruling files.** Each must be a regular UTF-8 file with a visible character: a FIFO, a directory, an absent
  file, a blank file and non-UTF-8 bytes are all refused before the precheck, exit 2. Name each for nothing it says,
  since its path is printed on the INVOCATION line. The text is recorded in the checkpoint only, never printed.
- **The RULING line.** It prints before the load (after the DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines) and names each text's sha16 (of
  the stripped text) and what it is offered for: the pause's kind and launch count, or the checkpoint's state. Its form
  is `RULING campaign-Q-F0: the seat's adjudication (sha16 X) offered for the pause at 40 launches (look)`. The report
  gives a pause's recorded ruling (the seat's, or the PI's stop where it is that pause's ruling, offered without the
  seat's adjudication) the same sha16, and a later NO PAUSE TO ADJUDICATE refusal names it. The RULING line is the only
  record of a text's sha16 in two cases: the PI's stop where the stage records it only as the campaign's stop, and a
  ruling offered beside an end pending that the checkpoint does not record, which the stage does not apply there (NOT
  APPLIED). Both are declared (the review's B2-3, F3-7 and RM4-1).
- **A campaign checkpoint the driver cannot read** (not a regular file; not a JSON object; a shape or value no write of
  the package makes) is refused before the load (exit 2, `checkpoint: ... cannot be read as a campaign checkpoint` or
  `... is not a regular file`). No write of the package makes one, so it is an incident: escalate it, and never edit
  the checkpoint.
- **The ruling-fit refusals before the load** (exit 2, `ruling: ...`) are the stage's own, made before the 27B load
  rather than after it:
  - an adjudication with no unadjudicated pause (NO PAUSE TO ADJUDICATE; where the last pause already carries a
    ruling, the message names that pause and its ruling's sha16 and says how to resume: with `--resume` alone, or,
    where the PI's stop is offered beside it, with `--resume` and the PI's stop alone);
  - a PI stop before any launch (PI STOP REFUSED);
  - an adjudication on an exhausted schedule without the PI's stop (SCHEDULE EXHAUSTED);
  - a PI stop text other than the one the checkpoint records (CHECKPOINT MISMATCH).
- **An adjudication is offered only for the pause it rules on.** Check the pause before every invocation that offers
  one: the seat's ruling should name the pause it rules on (ask if it does not); the report's pauses (kind,
  at_launches) or a no-ruling invocation's PAUSE line give its kind and launch count, and look=k in a look's BAR
  messages is the look at 40k launches. Afterwards the invocation's RULING line confirms what the ruling
  was offered for; it prints in the same invocation, before the load, so it cannot be checked beforehand.
- **After an interrupted invocation that offered a ruling:**
  - the seat's adjudication alone: re-run with `--resume` alone. A recorded adjudication resumes the campaign, or
    re-finishes an end its finish left pending (where that finish had completed, the re-run shows the new pause, or is
    refused as ended); an unrecorded one shows the pause again -- offer it again only if the shown pause is the one it
    rules on and the interrupted invocation's RULING line did not say "beside an end pending" (then never: the bullet
    below). The stage records it at the invocation's first write, which comes just after the
    PROVENANCE line (a kill between the two leaves it unrecorded).
  - the PI's stop (alone or beside the seat's adjudication): re-run with `--resume` and the same ruling files (where
    its RULING line said "beside an end pending", with the PI's stop alone: the next bullet). Recorded or not, the
    campaign ends PI-STOP, save that an end left pending stands where it is a terminal end or a STOP (re-finished
    first, or, where its re-finish had completed, refused as ended before the load), and where it is a look's or an
    exhausted schedule's pause its re-finish leaves the stop NOT APPLIED there, to be offered again (the next bullet).
    Should NO PAUSE TO ADJUDICATE then refuse the adjudication, it was recorded: drop `--adjudication-file`, as the
    refusal itself says. Should the run report NOT APPLIED instead -- both rulings were offered and the interrupted
    invocation was killed in its own finish (the adjudication was recorded and needs nothing), or it found an end
    pending and was killed before its re-finish completed -- the next bullet applies.
- **Beside an end pending** (a finish interrupted; the RULING line of an invocation that finds one says "beside an end
  pending"; one killed in its own finish found none, and its RULING line named the pause it was offered for, or the
  checkpoint's state): the end is re-finished first, and a ruling offered with it -- the seat's adjudication, or a PI
  stop the checkpoint does not record -- is NOT APPLIED (exit 4). So beside one no adjudication is offered, and the
  PI's stop, if offered, alone (a stop recorded with that end re-finishes with no refusal). An adjudication offered
  beside one is never offered again, whatever NOT APPLIED's "offer it again" says: it rules on no pause shown when it
  was offered (it is the previous pause's ruling, already recorded, or one written before the pause it would rule on
  was shown). Then:
  - a re-finished pause that leaves the campaign open is now shown and takes the seat's own ruling on it, or the PI's
    stop offered again: a look's, a new `--adjudication-file`; an exhausted schedule's, the PI's stop (the seat's
    ruling only beside it);
  - after a terminal end or a STOP, that end stands: a pause it carries takes the seat's own ruling on it, once shown,
    through VERDICTS (the declared `--adjudication` file), and a PI's stop on a hair trigger the declared `--pi-stop`
    record, as NOT APPLIED's message says;
  - an adjudication the checkpoint already records needs nothing (the report's adjudication_sha16 equals the RULING
    line's).
- **NOT APPLIED** (exit 4) is the stage declining a ruling offered beside an end pending, after it has re-finished that
  end (stages.py:2503-2520): the seat's adjudication, or a PI stop the checkpoint does not record. What follows is the
  bullet above -- its "offer it again" holds for the PI's stop only. The NOT APPLIED line is not itself a stage's STOP;
  where the end it re-finished is a STOP (announced before it), that STOP is escalated as any STOP.

- **Declared limits** (the review's, as the review raw's header states them):

  - VC3-5: the self-test pins the re-run guidance in the campaign's help (camp_pauses and the two flags' help); the
    module docstring, _run_stage's docstring and the handoff README agree with it by review, unpinned.
  - VC4-1 and VC4-2, for the driver's next edit: its PI's-stop re-run sentence names only the exception of a
    terminal end or a STOP -- where the end left pending is a look's or an exhausted schedule's pause, the stop is
    NOT APPLIED there and offered again, and an adjudication recorded beside a recorded stop meets an ended-kind NOT

    does not name a finish completed before the kill (the re-run then shows the new pause, or is refused as ended);
    the "Beside an end pending" sentence governs these cases, and README §3 states them. The module docstring's reason
    for never re-offering an adjudication offered beside an end pending is exact in its first alternative only; the
    rule stands.
  - The interpreter: the cloud's runs are on Python 3.13, the self-test of record on the laptop's 3.12.9; and the
    canary's reading of laptop paths is bounded by scrub() (the repository, the temp root and sys.executable
    replaced), the reader's 5-digit floor and its letter boundaries -- a residual risk the self-test of record tests
    (round 4's operations lens). Should the self-test of record fail the canary check on the laptop, that is the
    class: report it before the launch, and launch nothing.
- **Not built, for the seat if it wants them:** round 1's O1-3 option, a refusal before the load of a ruling whose
  stripped text's sha16 equals an earlier pause's recorded one (it needs the seat's word; the RULING line and the
  report's per-pause digests make a reuse visible, and it would refuse values-check pass 2's VC2-1 scenario, a stale
  adjudication re-offered for a pause it does not rule on, which the driver's guidance now forbids); RM4-1's option,
  a RULING-line note when an offered adjudication equals the one the checkpoint records; the operations refuter's
  alternative to O4-1, a refusal before the load of a ruling offered beside an end pending (it would save the 27B
  load; the next invocation re-finishes either way).
- **The self-test of record:** the laptop's result, in the entry.
---- campaign_dry_precheck.py, verbatim
"""Laptop only, offline and model-free: the campaign's pre-load checks on the COMMITTED driver, as
scripts/m_sessions.py's _run_stage makes them before the Qwen load, without loading anything.

Not of record (wip handoff). Run from the repository root, in the jlens venv, with HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 and the
audit session idle (F1), BEFORE the launch and on the same tree:

    HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 python -I -B <this file> <repo> --analyzer-sha256 H --package-sha256 H --renders-sha16 H

The analyzer's and the package's sha256 as LOCK-m-F0 v1.0 §1 names them, the renders of record's sha16 as lock §0
names it ("How a stage cites this file": `a04f67b0e5105a05`); the proposal is PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md and
the lock LOCK-m-F0.md, both committed. It prints, in order:
  1. the identity's problems (stage_identity_extra('campaign'));
  2. the licence's (unpatched);
  3. stage_precheck's, with no --resume (a first launch) and the rulings _run_stage passes when no ruling file is
     given ({'adjudication': None, 'pi_stop': None});
  4. the driver check's and the snapshot check's;
  5. the revision of record's comparison, whenever the snapshot check is clear ("revision of record: refs/main is
     <rev>, <equal | NOT ...>"; with a snapshot problem it stops after item 4, exit 1);
  6. the DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines exactly as the run would print them;
  7. a "campaign values:" line: the driver's launch counts, floors and hour cap, and the lock's line forms it tests.
Every problem list passes through the driver's redact_totals (the step-14 read, Q3) before it is printed. Exit 0 iff
every list is empty, the revision is the revision of record and both lines formed. It opens nothing under
data/m/ckpt or data/m/reports itself; stage_precheck reads data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_qual.json (the prior gate, through the
package) and, where one exists, the campaign checkpoint (its checkpoint checks and the driver's screen), as the run
will, and it prints only their problems."""
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("repo")
ap.add_argument("--analyzer-sha256", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--package-sha256", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--renders-sha16", required=True)
a = ap.parse_args()
REPO = Path(a.repo).resolve()
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "scripts"))
import m_sessions as me                                    # noqa: E402
from valence_core.m import stages                          # noqa: E402

bad = False


def show(label, probs):
    global bad
    print(f"{label}: {me.redact_totals(repr(list(probs)))}", flush=True)
    bad |= bool(probs)


extra, probs = me.stage_identity_extra("campaign", "PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md", me.TIME_LOCK_FILE,
                                       analyzer_sha256=a.analyzer_sha256, package_sha256=a.package_sha256,
                                       renders_sha16=a.renders_sha16)
show("stage_identity_extra('campaign')", probs)
if extra is None:
    sys.exit(1)
show("licence (unpatched)", stages.licence_problems(extra))
show("stage_precheck('campaign')", me.stage_precheck("campaign", extra, resume_run=False,
                                                     out_root=Path(stages.DEFAULT_OUT),
                                                     rulings={"adjudication": None, "pi_stop": None}))
dprobs, dsha = me.driver_problems()
show("driver_problems", dprobs)
sprobs, snap = me.qwen_snapshot()
show("qwen_snapshot", sprobs)
if snap is not None:
    same = snap["revision"] == me.QWEN_REVISION
    print(f"revision of record: refs/main is {snap['revision']}, "
          + ("equal to the revision of record" if same else f"NOT the revision of record {me.QWEN_REVISION}"),
          flush=True)
    bad |= not same
if dsha is None or snap is None:
    sys.exit(1)
print(f"DRIVER {me.DRIVER_REL} {dsha}: committed at HEAD {stages._git_head()}, working tree identical", flush=True)
print(me.snapshot_line(snap), flush=True)
print(f"campaign values: launch counts {dict(me.CAMPAIGN_LAUNCH_COUNTS)}, floors {me.campaign_floors()}, "
      f"hour cap {me.CAMPAIGN_HOUR_CAP} (lines {list(me.CAMPAIGN_LOCK_LINES)})", flush=True)
sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

---- prelaunch16.sh, verbatim
#!/bin/zsh
# The Q-F0 campaign's pre-launch checks (Code's; no python, no model): the audit session's state (F1) and the
# step-14 conditions (O2-5): no batch, no reviewer or self-test process, no model python. Nothing under data/m is
# listed (O2-3: data/m and the raw are excluded from the mtime scan).
set -u
R=/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab
cd $R || exit 9
echo "== processes"
echo "batches: [$(pgrep -fl 'xargs|runst1[46]|runmut1[46]|job.sh' | tr '\n' ';')]"
echo "python (jlens|mlx|qwen|ipykernel|m_sessions|selftest): [$(pgrep -ifl python | /usr/bin/grep -iE 'jlens|mlx|qwen|ipykernel|m_sessions|selftest' | tr '\n' ';')]"
echo "python (all): [$(pgrep -ifl python | cut -c1-120 | tr '\n' ';')]"
echo "== the audit session (F1)"
echo "last commit touching its paths: $(git --no-optional-locks log -1 --format='%h %ci %s' -- 'scripts/audit_note_*' 'results/raw/*note-*' README-NOTE.md 'RESEARCH-NOTE-*' 'AUDIT-note-*' | cut -c1-160)"
echo "last commit overall: $(git --no-optional-locks log -1 --format='%h %ci %an' | cut -c1-120)"
echo "files modified in the last 30 min (excluding .git, jlens-qwen36, data/m, results/raw/*campaign-qf0.txt):"
find . \( -path ./.git -o -path ./jlens-qwen36 -o -path ./data/m \) -prune -o -type f -mmin -30 -print \
  | /usr/bin/grep -v 'results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-qf0.txt' | head -20
echo "git status (tracked, modified):"
git --no-optional-locks status --short | /usr/bin/grep -v '^??' | head -20
echo "== repo root"
ls -d mlx-community 2>&1 | head -1

6. THE HEADER'S GENERATOR (gen_header16.py; step 14's gen_header14.py, recovered from the transcript, as the template) and its output (the header it wrote to the raw, and the command to type)

---- gen_header16.py, verbatim
"""The Q-F0 campaign's launch raw: the header lines, written just before the launch (Code's; step 14's generator,
gen_header14.py, as the template, with the handoff README's §2 values and the PI's two words of 2026-10-18). No finish
stamp anywhere (the seat's step-10 read, Q4; v1.0 §12). Every value it states is read or asserted here, not typed
(round 3's O3-5), except the audit session's state and the PI's words, which are passed in. Usage:
/usr/bin/python3 -I gen_header16.py RAW EXPECTED_DRIVER_SHA256 DRY_PRECHECK_OUT AUDIT_LINE PI_LAUNCH_WORD PI_PAUSE_WORD
Writes RAW (refused if it exists; it must be absolute, under results/raw) and prints the header and the exact command
to type (the checklist line plus `; echo "PID=$!"`, so the PID the waits use comes from the same call; O3-6)."""
import hashlib, os, re, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path
R = Path("/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab")
raw, want_drv, dry_out, audit, w_launch, w_pause = (Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2], Path(sys.argv[3]), sys.argv[4],
                                                    sys.argv[5], sys.argv[6])
assert raw.is_absolute() and raw.parent == R / "results" / "raw" and not raw.exists(), raw   # O3-10
sh = lambda p: hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
run = lambda *a: subprocess.run(list(a), capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
git = lambda *a: run("git", "--no-optional-locks", "-C", str(R), *a)
blob = lambda rel: hashlib.sha256(subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(R), "show", f"HEAD:{rel}"], capture_output=True,
                                                 check=True).stdout).hexdigest()
head = git("rev-parse", "HEAD"); hshort = head[:7]
assert git("status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no") == "", "tracked changes in the tree"
# the driver: the reviewed blob, committed at HEAD as it is in the working tree (O3-4; README §2)
drv = sh(R / "scripts/m_sessions.py")
assert drv == want_drv == blob("scripts/m_sessions.py") == \
    "609bb469d97ca40c0f5cc1592902976019f16b2c09b14961fe8352ecaf932015", (drv, want_drv)
drv_commit = git("log", "-1", "--format=%h", "--", "scripts/m_sessions.py")
assert drv_commit == "a6c81f2", drv_commit
# the lock: v1.0's, untouched since d474406 (README §2: the identity check reads the committed blob)
LOCK = "fc7e9db8ecfa28d61b1ce1765d869000fbf827dcb013cd1d65220c9e85e0015c"
lock = sh(R / "LOCK-m-F0.md")
assert lock == LOCK == blob("LOCK-m-F0.md"), lock
assert git("log", "--format=%h", "d474406..HEAD", "--", "LOCK-m-F0.md") == "", "a commit after d474406 touches the lock"
# the package and the analyzer, as v1.0 §1 names them; nothing under valence_core changed since d474406
PKG = "5f646db6e82c5561ff8a425e91beb5454068a381564894e93d19b9165459d771"
ANA = "655e0f7f6743d0bf497f5ba97ce37f7febf9fcf6a45e068b5385ee945c5dc16f"
REN = "a04f67b0e5105a05"
assert sh(R / "scripts/m_analyze.py") == ANA == blob("scripts/m_analyze.py")
assert git("diff", "--name-only", "d474406", "HEAD", "--", "valence_core", "scripts/m_analyze.py", "CLAUDE.md",
           "PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md") == ""
lock_text = (R / "LOCK-m-F0.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
assert ANA in lock_text and PKG in lock_text and REN in lock_text
assert "launch counts = A0 128, A0s 64" in lock_text and "hour cap = 40 h" in lock_text
assert not (R / "mlx-community").exists() and not (R / "mlx-community").is_symlink()
for p in ("data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_campaign.json", "data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_campaign_adjudication.json",
          "data/m/reports/Q-F0_campaign_report.json"):
    assert not (R / p).exists(), f"{p} exists before the first launch"
# the snapshot, read from the hub cache (O3-5): refs/main, the snapshot directories, the directory
MID, REV = "mlx-community/Qwen3.6-27B-4bit", "c000ac2c2057d94be3fa931000c31723aac53282"
cache = Path(os.environ.get("HF_HUB_CACHE") or Path.home() / ".cache/huggingface/hub")
repo_dir = cache / ("models--" + MID.replace("/", "--"))
refs_main = (repo_dir / "refs/main").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
snaps = sorted(p.name for p in (repo_dir / "snapshots").iterdir())
snapdir = repo_dir / "snapshots" / REV
assert refs_main == REV and snaps == [REV] and snapdir.is_dir(), (refs_main, snaps)
# the dry precheck's output on this driver (campaign_dry_precheck.py, the wip handoff's): every list [], the revision
# of record, the DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines, the campaign values, EXIT=0
dry = dry_out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for lab in ("stage_identity_extra('campaign')", "licence (unpatched)", "stage_precheck('campaign')",
            "driver_problems", "qwen_snapshot"):
    assert f"{lab}: []\n" in dry, f"dry precheck: {lab}"
assert f"revision of record: refs/main is {REV}, equal to the revision of record\n" in dry, "dry revision"
assert f"DRIVER scripts/m_sessions.py {drv}: committed at HEAD {head}, working tree identical\n" in dry, "dry DRIVER"
assert (f"SNAPSHOT {MID} {REV}: refs/main; {snapdir}; 1 snapshot(s) in the model's cache repo; HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\n"
        in dry), "dry SNAPSHOT"
assert ("campaign values: launch counts {'A0': 128, 'A0s': 64}, floors {'A0': 80, 'A0s': 32}, hour cap 40 (lines "
        "['launch counts = A0 128, A0s 64', 'hour cap = 40 h'])\n") in dry, "dry campaign values"
assert dry.rstrip("\n").endswith("EXIT=0"), "dry EXIT"
# the self-test of record on this driver, both conditions (README §1 item 1): 825/825, EXIT=0

for f in ("selftest_run1_unset.txt", "selftest_run2_offline.txt"):

    assert st[-2:] == ["  825/825 m selftest checks pass", "EXIT=0"] and not any("[FAIL]" in x for x in st), f
# the inputs no check binds (OPS-6, O3-8): the clone's closure, asserted equal to G-ID-F0m's header; the adapter
# files outside package_sha256, committed and identical to HEAD; qwen_mlx.py equal to lock §5.2's value
clone = ["model.py", "patch_gdn.py", "custom_gdn_patch.py", "custom_gdn_vjp.py", "gdn_backward.py", "conditioning.py",
         "forced_choice.py"]
gid_hdr = (R / "results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gid-f0m.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")[4]
cl = {f: sh(R / "jlens-qwen36/jlens_qwen" / f) for f in clone}
assert all(f"{f} {h}" in gid_hdr for f, h in cl.items()), "the clone closure differs from G-ID-F0m's header"
adp = {f: sh(R / "valence_core/adapters" / f) for f in ("qwen_mlx.py", "__init__.py", "base.py")}
assert all(adp[f] == blob(f"valence_core/adapters/{f}") for f in adp)
assert adp["qwen_mlx.py"] == "a3fc7b45749175239a88bf1cf6ff64190568a72eacf5a2e301a1e04e11d52c6b"   # lock §5.2
# the checklist, read now (m:267): power, the one-process state, disk, macOS
watt = re.search(r"Wattage \(W\): (\d+)", run("system_profiler", "SPPowerDataType")).group(1)

assert int(watt) >= 96 and "AC Power" in batt, (watt, batt)

hits = [l for l in py if l and re.search(r"jlens|mlx|qwen|ipykernel|m_sessions|selftest", l, re.I)]
assert not hits, hits
batches = subprocess.run(["pgrep", "-fl", "xargs|runst1[46]|runmut1[46]|job.sh"], capture_output=True,
                         text=True).stdout.strip()
assert not batches, batches
df = run("df", "-h", "/").split("\n")[1].split()
macos = run("sw_vers", "-productVersion") + " (" + run("sw_vers", "-buildVersion") + ")"
stamp = run("date", "-u", "+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
line = (f"nohup caffeinate -is /bin/zsh -c 'cd {R} && export HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && {R}/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python "
        f"{R}/scripts/m_sessions.py campaign --unit Q-F0 --proposal-file PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md "

        f"echo \"EXIT=$?\"' >> {raw} 2>&1 & disown")
typed = line + '; echo "PID=$!"'
P = "'[m]_sessions\\.py campaign'"

    if cid in seen: continue

    print(n, "|", s.replace("\n", " ")[:170])

#!/bin/zsh
# After an invocation of the Q-F0 campaign has ended (the PID loop returned; pgrep re-checked here): the EXIT lines
# counted, the last EXIT and the STAGE and output-path lines printed (no figures), and from the pick-free report only
# its status, the last pause's kind and launch count, the pause count, and the identity's lock and schedule sha256.
# No other line of the raw is printed; nothing under data/m/ckpt is opened. No size or time metadata.
set -u
R=/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab; cd $R || exit 9
RAW=results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-qf0.txt
n=$(pgrep -f '[m]_sessions\.py campaign' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
if [ "$n" != 0 ]; then echo "STILL RUNNING: $n process(es)"; exit 8; fi
echo "EXIT lines: $(/usr/bin/grep -c '^EXIT=' $RAW)"
echo "last EXIT: $(/usr/bin/grep '^EXIT=' $RAW | tail -1)"
echo "STAGE lines: [$(/usr/bin/grep '^STAGE campaign-Q-F0 ' $RAW | tr '\n' ';')]"
echo "output lines: [$(/usr/bin/grep -E '^(CHECKPOINT|REPORT|ADJUDICATION) ' $RAW | tr '\n' ';')]"
for f in data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_campaign.json data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_campaign_adjudication.json data/m/reports/Q-F0_campaign_report.json; do
  test -e $f && echo "present $f" || echo "absent $f"; done
test -e data/m/reports/Q-F0_campaign_report.json && /usr/bin/python3 -I -c '
import json
r = json.load(open("data/m/reports/Q-F0_campaign_report.json", encoding="utf-8"))
ps = r.get("pauses") or []
last = ps[-1] if ps else None
print("report status:", r.get("status"), "| pauses:", len(ps), "| last pause:",
      None if last is None else (last["kind"], last["at_launches"], "adjudicated" if last["adjudicated"] else "unadjudicated"))
t = r.get("terminal")
print("terminal:", None if t is None else t.get("status"))
i = r["identity"]
print("identity lock_sha256 == v1.0:", i["lock_sha256"] == "fc7e9db8ecfa28d61b1ce1765d869000fbf827dcb013cd1d65220c9e85e0015c",
      "| schedule_sha256 == lock s6:", i["schedule_sha256"] == "c4626cc01060529a6f7cc6ebfd41dec272de06527f18ae1d6b45d332e51e303e",
      "| path:", i["path"])
'

import json, sys
calls = []
for ln in open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8"):
    try: d = json.loads(ln)
    except Exception: continue
    c = (d.get("message") or {}).get("content")
    if d.get("type") != "assistant" or not isinstance(c, list): continue
    for x in c:
        if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("type") == "tool_use":
            i = x.get("input") or {}
            calls.append((x["id"], x["name"], i.get("command") or i.get("file_path") or json.dumps(i)[:120]))
seen, uniq = set(), []
for cid, n, s in calls:
    if cid in seen: continue
    seen.add(cid); uniq.append((n, s))
a = max(k for k, (n, s) in enumerate(uniq) if n == "Bash" and s.startswith("nohup caffeinate -is /bin/zsh -c"))
b = min(k for k, (n, s) in enumerate(uniq) if k > a and n == "Bash" and "step16launch/after_exit.sh" in s and s.startswith("zsh "))
for n, s in uniq[a:b + 1]:
    print(n, "|", s.replace("\n", " ")[:170])

---- extract_entry.py, verbatim
"""The campaign raw's printed record for the launch entry (Code's; read after the invocation ended). Prints:
the non-session, non-header lines verbatim, save the PROVENANCE line, whose digits are masked (its start stamp is
one of Q4's unavoidable records; nothing is computed from it); and the session lines tallied. A sham (A0s) session's
printed status is tallied in full. A contrast (A0) session's is tallied as m:263/m:271 let an entry carry it: its
forced-round (pre-pick) exclusions by class, and every other status ('valid' or 'excluded:excluded@free') pooled as one
count, so no contrast free-round event is counted or shown. Also checks the s= indices and the arm rotation."""
import re, sys
from collections import Counter
raw = open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8").read().split("\n")
body = [l for l in raw if not l.startswith("#")]
SES = re.compile(r"^campaign (A0|A0s) s=(\d+) (.+)$")
sess, other = [], []
for l in body:
    m = SES.match(l)
    (sess.append(m.groups()) if m else other.append(l)) if l.startswith("campaign ") else other.append(l)
print("== non-session lines (in order)")
for l in other:
    if l.startswith("PROVENANCE"):
        print(re.sub(r"\d", "#", l)[:600], "  [digits masked]")
    elif l.strip():
        print(l)
odd = [l for l in body if l.startswith("campaign ") and not SES.match(l)]
print("== session lines:", len(sess), "| campaign-prefixed lines not of the session form:", len(odd))
for a in ("A0", "A0s"):
    idx = [int(s) for arm, s, _ in sess if arm == a]
    print(f"  {a}: launched {len(idx)}; s= indices 0..{len(idx)-1} in order: {idx == list(range(len(idx)))}")
sham = Counter(st for arm, _, st in sess if arm == "A0s")
print("  A0s printed statuses:", dict(sorted(sham.items())))
con = [st for arm, _, st in sess if arm == "A0"]
forced = Counter(st for st in con if re.fullmatch(r"excluded:\w+@forced", st))
rest = [st for st in con if not re.fullmatch(r"excluded:\w+@forced", st)]
assert all(st in ("valid", "excluded:excluded@free") for st in rest), "an A0 status outside m:263's three forms"
print("  A0 forced-round (pre-pick) exclusions by printed class:", dict(sorted(forced.items())),
      "| A0 sessions not excluded before the pick (valid or excluded@free, pooled):", len(rest))
arms = [arm for arm, _, _ in sess]
print("  arm sequence, run-length:", [(k, sum(1 for _ in g)) for k, g in __import__("itertools").groupby(arms)][:12], "...")

"""The campaign raw's printed record for the launch entry (Code's; read after the invocation ended). Prints:

count, so no contrast free-round event is counted or shown. Also checks the s= indices and the arm rotation."""

    m = SES.match(l)
    (sess.append(m.groups()) if m else other.append(l)) if l.startswith("campaign ") else other.append(l)
